Their core products include:\\n\\nEmpathic Voice Interface (EVI): This is a speech-language model that handles transcription, language understanding, and speech generation. EVI aims to bring more expressiveness, realism, and emotional understanding to voice AI. EVI 3 is the latest iteration, capable of streaming user speech and forming natural, expressive responses with low latency (~300ms). A key feature of EVI 3 is its ability to speak with any voice and personality created with a prompt, moving beyond a limited set of speakers. It analyzes the tune, rhythm, and timbre of user speech to understand emotional cues and can generate authentic emotional expressions on command. EVI 3 also integrates real-time information through context injection and can connect to external systems and databases. It supports advanced turn-taking, flexible LLM options (built-in, user's own, or other providers), voice customization without voice cloning, comprehensive analytics, phone calling deployment, and memory capabilities. EVI 3 is available in preview via their iOS app and the API is coming soon.
